# RunWear outfit engine v4.2 — full scenario sweep

**Date:** 2026-10-17 · **Engine:** `GetOutfitRecommendationUseCase`, `ENGINE_VERSION = "4.2"` ·
**Harness:** `OutfitRulesSweepTest` in `RunWear-android/shared/src/test/java/com/runwear/shared/domain/usecase/` ·
**Machine report:** [outfit-rules-sweep-2026-10-17-full-report.txt](outfit-rules-sweep-2026-10-17-full-report.txt) (committed alongside this doc)

## Verdict

**The v4.2 fix is proven across the engine's entire input space.** The failure Sol hit in
New York — 47°F actual / 40°F feels-like / 11 mph producing three stacked torso layers —
cannot recur: in the 25–40°F band a windbreaker and a mid layer never co-occur (0 violations
in 24,000 band scenarios), both of Sol's field cases reproduce their shipped-correct results
exactly, and warmth layering is monotone, cliff-free beyond the allowed step, and wind-sane
everywhere (0 violations across ~585,000 chain comparisons).

**One genuine new finding (inv7):** the comfort preference also moves *sun protection*.
The cap/visor thresholds in `selectHead()` read the comfort-**adjusted** temperature, so in
high UV at 40–59°F feels-like, choosing a warmer comfort profile can *add* a running cap —
the warmer profile ends up with **more** items than the colder one. 640 scenarios invert the
expected profile ordering, at every one of the four comfort steps. Warmth layering itself
never inverts: strip the sun gear out and the ordering holds in all 640. Whether this is a
bug is a design call (sun gear keyed off adjusted rather than actual temperature) — it is
reported here, not fixed. **This finding keeps `:shared:testDebugUnitTest` red by design**
(13 tests, 12 pass, `inv7_profileOrdering` fails) until someone decides.

> **Resolved 2026-10-19:** the owner decided — sun gear is not layering and keys to the
> RAW temperature. Fixed in engine v4.3; harness green end-to-end (13/13, 0 inv7
> inversions). See [outfit-rules-sweep-v43-addendum-2026-10-19.md](outfit-rules-sweep-v43-addendum-2026-10-19.md).

Everything else below is passes and observations. No engine code was changed in this task.

## What was tested

- **Module:** the live project is `RunWear-android/` (its `:app` and `:wear` modules both
  depend on `:shared`). A nested stale copy exists at `RunWear-android/RunWear/` — an
  initial-commit snapshot whose engine has no `ENGINE_VERSION` and which received none of
  the v4.2 commits (verified via git history). It was **not** swept.
- **Reproduce:** `gradle :shared:testDebugUnitTest` (JDK 17, Gradle 8.10,
  `GRADLE_USER_HOME=~/workspace/.gradle-runwear`). The sweep writes its full report to
  `shared/build/outfit-sweep/report.txt` on every run.
- **Pre-existing tests:** `:shared` had **no unit tests at all** before this harness (no
  `src/test` existed), so the 13 tests above are the module's whole suite: 12 pass, 1 fails
  (inv7). The `:app` module's tests were not run — out of scope for an engine sweep.

## The grid

Main grid: **209,600 scenarios**, the full cross product of the engine's real inputs.
Nothing was trimmed.

| Axis | Values |
|---|---|
| Feels-like | −20…110 °F in 1° steps (131) — actual temperature equal to feels-like |
| Wind | 0, 3, 6, 9, 12, 16, 20, 25, 30, 40 mph (engine reads it only via `isWindy = windSpeed > 10`) |
| Condition | DRY · DRIZZLE (`isRaining`, precip 0.05) · RAIN (`isRaining`, precip 0.5) · SNOW (`isSnowing`, precip 0.4) |
| Comfort profile | All 5: Runs Very Cold (−10) · Runs Cold (−5) · Neutral (0) · Runs Warm (+5) · Runs Very Warm (+10) |
| Humidity | 30 / 85 (engine reads it only via `isHumid`, which feeds tips only — swept anyway) |
| Environment (hour × UV) | DAY_SUN (12:00, UV 8) · DAY_GREY (12:00, UV 0) · DAWN_UV (06:00, UV 8 — reflective gear and high-UV gear co-occur; structural-max probe) · NIGHT (21:00, UV 0) |

Plus two slices: **divergence** (actual = feels ±10 °F; 2,620 checks, inv10) and
**Celsius round-trip** (feels-like supplied in °C; 810 checks, inv11).

Inputs the engine never reads (source-verified, and measured in inv9/inv10): `temperature`,
`weatherCode`, `windGusts`, `windDirection`, `cloudCover`, `precipitationProbability`,
`isSnowing`.

## Results by invariant

| # | Invariant | Checks | Violations |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | 25–40 °F band: wind shell + mid layer never co-occur | 24,000 band scenarios | **0** (5,040 documented substitutions observed) |
| 2 | Monotonic warmth: torso layers never increase as feels-like rises 1 °F | 208,000 steps | **0** |
| 3 | No cliffs: adjacent 1 °F steps change total items by ≤ 2 | 208,000 steps | **0** over the limit; 800 jumps of exactly 2 (one pattern, §Cliffs) |
| 4 | Structure: never empty, exactly one bottom, ≥ 1 torso item, total 2–10 | 209,600 scenarios | **0** (observed totals 2…10) |
| 5 | No contradictory pairs (25 pairs from the item taxonomy, §inv5) | 209,600 scenarios | **0** |
| 6 | Wind sanity: more wind never yields fewer items | 188,640 wind steps / 20,960 rows | **0** decreases (840 shell-for-mid substitutions at 0→20 mph, all equal-count) |
| 7 | Profile ordering: runs-cold ≥ neutral ≥ runs-warm item count | 167,680 comparisons | **640 inversions — see §inv7** |
| 8 | Field-case regressions (Sol's two cases) | 2 cases | **0** — both exact (§Field cases) |
| 9 | Rain ⇒ rain jacket present | 52,400 rain scenarios | **0** missing |
| 10 | (extra) Actual temperature does not affect the outfit | 2,620 checks | **0** mismatches |
| 11 | (extra) Celsius inputs match Fahrenheit inputs | 810 checks | **0** mismatches |

### inv1 — the v4.2 exclusion, verified

In the adjusted 25–40 °F band the windbreaker/mid-layer stack never occurs (0 / 24,000).
The substitution the fix intended fires constantly: 5,040 band scenarios show the mid layer
dropped because the shell was called. Two framings worth keeping straight:

- **Rain stacks (observation, 2,800 scenarios):** a *rain jacket* over a mid layer does
  occur inside the band — e.g. feels 31 °F, neutral, rain → Light Long Sleeve +
  Quarter-Zip + Rain Jacket (torso = 3). The v4.2 exclusion in `execute()` names only
  `WINDBREAKER`; the rain branch returns before it. A waterproof shell over insulation in
  cold rain is plausibly intended — but it is outside the letter of the v4.2 rule, so it is
  recorded here rather than silently passed.
- **Raw-band stacks are comfort shifts, not leaks (3,600 scenarios):** if you read the raw
  thermometer (feels-like 25–40 °F) you can still see fleece + windbreaker — always when a
  runs-cold profile has shifted the *adjusted* temperature below 25 °F, where stacking is
  the designed cold outfit (e.g. feels 29 °F, Runs Very Cold → adjusted 19 °F → Thermal
  Long Sleeve + Fleece + Windbreaker). The band rule is defined on adjusted temperature;
  no scenario violates it on that definition.

### inv7 — the one failure: comfort shifts move sun gear

640 strict inversions of the profile ordering (runs-cold count ≥ neutral ≥ runs-warm),
out of 167,680 adjacent comfort comparisons. Every single one is the same mechanism:

- **Where:** feels-like 40–59 °F, high UV only (DAY_SUN 320, DAWN_UV 320; zero in grey/night),
  dry or snow only (rain/drizzle mask it — the rain cap occupies the head slot anyway).
- **Shape:** the warmer profile has exactly **+1 item** (totals 4<5 or 5<6), and the extra
  item is always the **Running Cap**: `selectHead()` returns `BASEBALL_CAP` when the
  *adjusted* temperature reaches 50 °F with UV > 3, so the warmer profile crosses the
  sun-gear threshold the colder profile doesn't reach. All four comfort steps invert
  somewhere (160 each: Very Cold→Cold, Cold→Neutral, Neutral→Warm, Warm→Very Warm).
- **Warmth is never the cause:** excluding sun gear (sunglasses, sunscreen, visor/cap),
  the ordering holds in all 640 — the harness verified this per inversion.

Example (verbatim from the machine report):

> feels=40F, wind=0, dry, humidity 30, DAY_SUN — RUNS_WARM (adjusted 45 °F): Light Long
> Sleeve, Running Shorts, Sunglasses, Sunscreen → total 4. RUNS_VERY_WARM (adjusted
> 50 °F): Short Sleeve Shirt, Running Shorts, **Running Cap**, Sunglasses, Sunscreen →
> total 5.

Judgment call left open: keying sun protection off the comfort-adjusted temperature means
"I run hot" can put a cap on your head and "I run cold" can take one off at the same
weather. The full list of all 640 inversions, with full inputs and outfits, is Appendix A.

### Cliffs — 800 jumps of exactly 2, one pattern

No adjacent 1 °F step anywhere changes the total by more than 2 (inv3 passes). The 800
jumps of exactly 2 are all the same event: **when the adjusted temperature crosses
40 °F upward, the Ear Warmer/Headband and the Light Gloves disappear in the same step** —
`selectHead()` and `selectHands()` share the ≥ 40 °F cutoff. Seen from the raw
thermometer that's feels steps 29→30 (Runs Very Warm), 34→35 (Runs Warm), 39→40
(Neutral),
44→45 (Runs Cold), 49→50 (Runs Very Cold) — 160 each, dry and snow only (rain/drizzle
keep a cap on the head), at every wind speed. A wearer crossing that line loses two
accessories at once; nothing else in the engine moves two items in one step. The complete
cliff list is Appendix B.

## Field cases (inv8) — both exact

**(a) Sol's case behind v4.2** — 47 °F actual / 40 °F feels-like / 11 mph / neutral / dry:
**Light Long Sleeve, Windbreaker, Light Tights — 3 items, 2 torso layers, no mid layer.**
(Pre-v4.2 this was 6 items with three torso layers.)

**(b) The shipped v4.2 reference case** — 39 °F feels-like / calm / neutral / dry:
**Light Long Sleeve, Light Tights, Ear Warmer/Headband, Light Gloves — 4 items, 1 torso
layer.** Matches the shipped result exactly.

## inv5 — contradiction pairs checked (25)

Derived from the `ClothingItem` taxonomy; zero co-occurrences in 209,600 scenarios.

- **Shorts / Short Shorts ×** Thermal Long Sleeve · Thermal Half-Zip · Insulated Jacket · Thermal Tights (8 pairs)
- **Tank Top/Singlet ×** Quarter-Zip · Fleece Pullover · Thermal Half-Zip · Windbreaker · Insulated Jacket · Light Tights · Thermal Tights · Light Gloves · Thermal Gloves · Mittens · Light Beanie · Thermal Beanie · Balaclava · Neck Gaiter (14 pairs)
- **Short Sleeve ×** Thermal Half-Zip · Insulated Jacket · Thermal Tights (3 pairs)

(RAIN_JACKET was deliberately excluded from the tank pairs: a waterproof shell over a
singlet in a hot downpour is legitimate gear. Same-slot pairs like Shorts × Thermal
Tights are structurally impossible — the model has one bottom slot — and were checked
anyway.)

## Observations (not invariant violations — recorded for the record)

1. **Drizzle gets no rain protection.** `selectTopOuter()` requires precipitation > 0.1,
   so with `isRaining` at precip 0.05 the rain jacket appears in **0 of 52,400** drizzle
   scenarios, at any temperature. (The head still gets a cap.) Whether real drizzle data
   ever arrives with precip ≤ 0.1 is an app-data question; the engine behavior is as coded.
2. **Snow does not exist to this engine.** Snow outfits are identical to their dry twins
   in **52,400 of 52,400** paired scenarios — `isSnowing` is never read by any selector.
3. **Rain overrides cold headgear.** `selectHead()` returns the Running Cap whenever
   `isRaining`, before the temperature brackets — so in rain at an adjusted −24 °F the
   outfit's head item is a baseball cap, not a balaclava (visible in the inv4 max-total
   examples in the machine report). Runners may accept a cap under a rain hood; at
   −24 °F it is at least worth a conscious decision.
4. **Celsius is clean on the sampled slice.** 810 °C/°F equivalence checks, 0 mismatches —
   the floating-point round-trip did not flip a bracket at any sampled point (feels in
   5 °F steps, so hairline cases between steps are not excluded by this slice).
5. **The actual-temperature field is decorative.** 2,620 divergence checks confirm the
   outfit depends only on feels-like; `WeatherConditions.temperature` reaches no selector.

## Process note — the first run's false alarm

The first sweep run reported 3,600 inv1 "violations." They were a **harness bug, not an
engine bug**: the grid builder called `execute(weather)` without its comfort argument, so
every scenario silently ran as NEUTRAL while the checks keyed brackets off each scenario's
own comfort shift. The tell was internal inconsistency — the "violating" outfits were
exactly what *raw* feels-like selects, ignoring the adjusted temperature entirely. Fixed
in commit `8a271fd`; runs 2 and 3 are identical and are what this doc reports. No results
from run 1 are used anywhere above.

## Not run / not done

- `:app` module unit tests — not run (outside the engine sweep's scope; `:shared` is the
  module under test and its full suite is reported above).
- No engine or app code changed. The inv7 finding and the observations are reported for
  a decision, per the task's no-fix instruction.
- Fractional feels-like values (e.g. 39.9 °F) are not in the grid — the grid steps whole
  degrees from −20 to 110. All engine brackets are whole-degree thresholds, and inv11
  found no round-trip flips on its slice, but a sub-degree sweep was not run.
